  "figures": [
    {
      "caption": "Main dependencies in the conditional proof of the signed momentum estimate. The dashed box is a bootstrap assumption. The first use of signed cancellation controls particle direction from this assumption and the baseline estimates; improved occupation is obtained afterward. The second use yields a strict improvement, and a continuity argument establishes the initially assumed bound.",
      "figure_id": "fig:dependencies",
      "pdf_page": 7,
      "source_evidence": [
        {
          "anchor": "fig:dependencies",
          "exact_quote": "Main dependencies in the conditional proof of the signed momentum estimate.\nThe dashed box is a bootstrap assumption. The first use of signed\ncancellation controls particle direction from this assumption and\nthe baseline estimates; improved",
          "source_object": "fig:0001"
        }
      ],
      "source_kind": "tikz_pgf",
      "tikz_pgf_source": "\\begin{tikzpicture}[\n  x=1cm,y=1cm,\n  stage/.style={draw=black!65,rounded corners=2pt,fill=white,\n    line width=.55pt,align=center,text width=4.6cm,\n    minimum height=.85cm,inner sep=5pt,font=\\small},\n  flow/.style={-{Stealth[length=2mm]},line width=.65pt,draw=black!75},\n  note/.style={font=\\footnotesize,fill=white,inner sep=2pt}\n]\n\\node[stage,dashed] (assume) at (0,0)\n  {Assumed increment bound\\\\up to time $\\tau$};\n\\node[stage] (identity) at (7,0)\n  {Signed cancellation\\\\along source labels};\n\\node[stage] (baseline) at (0,-1.5)\n  {Baseline occupation\\\\and direct estimates};\n\\node[stage] (direction) at (0,-3)\n  {Bound on changes\\\\of particle direction};\n\\node[stage] (occupation) at (0,-4.5)\n  {Improved occupation\\\\in the angular window};\n\\node[stage] (selected) at (7,-4.5)\n  {Selected-bin estimate\\\\for the signed force};\n\\node[stage] (strict) at (7,-6)\n  {Strict improvement\\\\of the assumed bound};\n\\node[stage] (close) at (0,-6)\n  {Continuity argument\\\\establishes the bound};\n\\draw[flow,dashed] (assume) -- (baseline);\n\\draw[flow] (baseline) -- (direction);\n\\draw[flow] (identity.south west) --\n  node[note,pos=.44,above,sloped] {first use} (direction.east);\n\\draw[flow] (identity) --\n  node[note,pos=.52,right] {second use} (selected);\n\\draw[flow] (direction) -- (occupation);\n\\draw[flow] (occupation) -- (selected);\n\\draw[flow] (selected) -- (strict);\n\\draw[flow] (strict) -- (close);\n\\end{tikzpicture}",
      "witness_description": "Flowchart of conditional dependencies. At top left, a dashed rectangle contains \"Assumed increment bound up to time $\\tau$\". Below it in a vertical column connected by downward arrows are three solid rectangles: \"Baseline occupation and direct estimates\", \"Bound on changes of particle direction\", and \"Improved occupation in the angular window\". At bottom left is a solid rectangle: \"Continuity argument establishes the bound\". At top right is a solid rectangle: \"Signed cancellation along source labels\". A diagonal arrow labeled \"first use\" points southwest from \"Signed cancellation along source labels\" to \"Bound on changes of particle direction\". A vertical downward arrow labeled \"second use\" points from \"Signed cancellation along source labels\" to a solid rectangle: \"Selected-bin estimate for the signed force\". A horizontal arrow points from \"Improved occupation in the angular window\" to \"Selected-bin estimate for the signed force\". A downward arrow points from \"Selected-bin estimate for the signed force\" to a solid rectangle: \"Strict improvement of the assumed bound\". A horizontal arrow points left from \"Strict improvement of the assumed bound\" to \"Continuity argument establishes the bound\"."
    },
    {
      "caption": "Retarded geometry along a receiver characteristic. The source event $(s,Y(s))$ lies on the backward light cone of $(t,X(t))$, with $r = t − s = |X(t)−Y(s)| > 0$. The spatial unit vector $n$ points from $Y(s)$ to $X(t)$. Panel (b) shows the source velocity $u(V(s)) = Y′(s)$ and the receiver velocity $a(t) = X′(t)$ at these respective events. The curves and vector directions are schematic.",
      "figure_id": "fig:retarded-geometry",
      "pdf_page": 12,
      "source_evidence": [
        {
          "anchor": "fig:retarded-geometry",
          "exact_quote": "Retarded geometry along a receiver characteristic.\n  The source event $(s,Y(s))$ lies on the backward light cone of\n  $(t,X(t))$, with $r=t-s=|X(t)-Y(s)|>0$.\n  The spatial unit vector $n$ points from $Y(s)$ to $X(t)$.\n  Panel~(b) shows the ",
          "source_object": "fig:0002"
        }
      ],
      "source_kind": "tikz_pgf",
      "tikz_pgf_source": "\\begin{tikzpicture}[\n      x=0.96cm,y=0.96cm,font=\\small,\n      >={Stealth[length=2mm]},\n      source/.style={draw=blue!65!black,line width=1pt},\n      receiver/.style={draw=orange!55!black,line width=1pt,densely dashed},\n      event/.style={circle,fill=black,inner sep=1.7pt},\n      guide/.style={draw=black!28,densely dotted},\n      labelbox/.style={fill=white,inner sep=1.5pt}\n    ]\n    \\node at (3.4,5.2) {\\textbf{(a)} Spacetime: schematic $1+1$ slice};\n    \\fill[blue!4] (0.55,0.9)--(3.55,3.9)--(6.55,0.9)--cycle;\n    \\draw[black!38,dashed] (0.55,0.9)--(3.55,3.9)--(6.55,0.9);\n    \\draw[->,black!65] (0.15,0.4)--(0.15,4.75) node[above] {time};\n    \\draw[->,black!65] (0.15,0.4)--(6.7,0.4) node[right] {space};\n    \\draw[guide] (0.15,2.05)--(1.7,2.05);\n    \\draw[guide] (0.15,3.9)--(3.55,3.9);\n    \\node[left] at (0.15,2.05) {$s$};\n    \\node[left] at (0.15,3.9) {$t$};\n    \\draw[source] (1.2,0.45)\n      .. controls (1.3,1.1) and (1.525,1.7) .. (1.7,2.05)\n      .. controls (2.0,2.65) and (2.12,3.6) .. (2.35,4.5);\n    \\draw[receiver] (2.8,0.45)\n      .. controls (2.86,1.45) and (2.9,2.2) .. (3.16,2.95)\n      .. controls (3.316,3.4) and (3.525,3.8) .. (3.55,3.9)\n      .. controls (3.6075,4.13) and (3.65,4.3) .. (3.72,4.5);\n    \\node[source,draw=none,anchor=east] at (2.17,4.42) {source $Y$};\n    \\node[receiver,draw=none,anchor=west] at (3.86,4.42) {receiver $X$};\n    \\draw[->,line width=1.1pt] (1.7,2.05)--(3.55,3.9);\n    \\node[event] at (1.7,2.05) {};\n    \\node[event] at (3.55,3.9) {};\n    \\node[anchor=south east,inner sep=0pt] at (1.60,2.23) {$(s,Y(s))$};\n    \\node[labelbox,anchor=west] at (3.7,3.87) {$(t,X(t))$};\n    \\node[align=center,black!60] at (4.85,1.42) {backward\\\\light cone};\n    \\node[labelbox,rotate=45] at (2.58,3.18) {light ray};\n\n    \\node at (10.85,5.2) {\\textbf{(b)} Spatial geometry};\n    \\node[black!65] at (10.85,4.68) {Positions at times $s$ and $t$};\n    \\draw[->,line width=1.1pt] (8.3,2.4)--(12.65,2.4)\n      node[midway,above=4pt] {$n$};\n    \\draw[source,->] (8.3,2.4)--(9.3,3.72)\n      node[above=3pt] {$u(V(s))$};\n    \\draw[receiver,->] (12.65,2.4)--(13.36,3.72)\n      node[above=3pt] {$a(t)$};\n    \\node[event] at (8.3,2.4) {};\n    \\node[event] at (12.65,2.4) {};\n    \\node[below=5pt] at (8.3,2.4) {$Y(s)$};\n    \\node[below=5pt] at (12.65,2.4) {$X(t)$};\n    \\draw[|<->|,black!65] (8.3,1.45)--(12.65,1.45)\n      node[midway,fill=white,inner sep=3pt] {$r$};\n    \\node at (10.7,0.66) {$\\displaystyle\n      r=t-s,\\qquad n=\\frac{X(t)-Y(s)}{r}$};\n  \\end{tikzpicture}",
      "witness_description": "Two diagrams side by side:\n(a) Spacetime: schematic 1 + 1 slice: Cartesian axes showing \"time\" on the vertical axis with tick marks $s$ and $t$, and \"space\" on the horizontal axis. A solid curved worldline labeled \"source $Y$\" passes through point $(s, Y(s))$. A dashed curved worldline labeled \"receiver $X$\" passes through point $(t, X(t))$. Dashed rays opening downward from $(t, X(t))$ indicate the \"backward light cone\". A straight dashed line labeled \"light ray\" connects $(s, Y(s))$ to $(t, X(t))$.\n(b) Spatial geometry: Subtitle \"Positions at times $s$ and $t$\". Two points $Y(s)$ and $X(t)$ are shown along a horizontal baseline. A vector arrow labeled $n$ points horizontally from $Y(s)$ to $X(t)$. A horizontal dimension line below indicates distance $r$. At $Y(s)$, an arrow labeled $u(V(s))$ points upward and rightward. At $X(t)$, a dashed arrow labeled $a(t)$ points upward and rightward. Below the geometry are formulas: $r = t - s$ and $n = \\frac{X(t) - Y(s)}{r}$."
    }
  ],
